Is that better than saving the rook?
If d4xe3, and then White plays Nxe8.
Black loses a rook and gains a bishop. (Rook = 5, Bishop = 3).
That's a net loss of 2 points.
So saving the rook is more important.

Therefore, `e8d8` is the move.

That doesn't save the rook.
What if Black moves the rook at f8?
That doesn't save the rook at e8.

The knight at f6 is at (f, 6).
The white king is at (e, 2).
The black pieces are King (h, 8), Rooks (e, 8), (f, 8), Bishop (d, 4).
No black piece is in a position to pin the knight at f6.

So `e8d8` is the most logical move.
